============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_chunk(documents: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tokenize and compute TF for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects in this chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to:\n        {\n            'doc_id': int,\n            'tf': dict[str, float],\n            'terms': set[str]\n        }\n    \"\"\"\n    partial_results = []\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_terms = set(tf.keys())\n\n        partial_results.append(\n            {\"doc_id\": doc.doc_id, \"tf\": tf, \"terms\": doc_terms}\n        )\n\n    return partial_results\n\n\ndef _compute_idf_chunk(\n    terms_chunk: list[str],\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int],\n    num_documents: int,\n) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute IDF values for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        terms_chunk: List of terms to compute IDF for\n        document_frequencies: Global document frequency counts\n        num_documents: Total number of documents\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to IDF score\n    \"\"\"\n    idf_dict = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = document_frequencies.get(term, 0)\n        if df > 0:\n            idf_dict[term] = math.log(num_documents / df) + 1\n\n    return idf_dict\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build posting lists for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf)\n            - terms_chunk: List of terms to build posting lists for\n            - doc_tf_dict: {doc_id: {term: tf}} lookup\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to posting list [(doc_id, tfidf_score), ...]\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf = args_tuple\n    posting_lists = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        posting_list = []\n\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_dict.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        posting_lists[term] = posting_list\n\n    return posting_lists\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (doc_tf_chunk, idf)\n            - doc_tf_chunk: {doc_id: {term: tf}} for documents in this chunk\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors, doc_norms) dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_chunk, idf = args_tuple\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_chunk.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list[list]:\n    \"\"\"Create chunks of specified size from a list.\"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = None,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes document-independent stages:\n    1. Tokenization and TF computation (parallel)\n    2. Document frequency aggregation (sequential)\n    3. IDF computation (parallel)\n    4. Posting list construction (parallel)\n    5. Document vectors and norms (parallel)\n    6. Merge results (sequential)\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 1.5x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        chunk_size: Documents per chunk (default: 500)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    # Validate inputs\n    if not documents:\n        index = TFIDFIndex()\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return ParallelIndexingResult(\n            index=index,\n            elapsed_time=elapsed,\n            num_documents=0,\n            vocabulary_size=0,\n        )\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results_list = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_chunk, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Flatten partial results\n    all_partials = []\n    for partial_chunk in partial_results_list:\n        all_partials.extend(partial_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 2: Sequential Document Frequency Aggregation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocabulary = set()\n    document_frequencies = defaultdict(int)\n\n    for partial in all_partials:\n        vocabulary.update(partial[\"terms\"])\n        for term in partial[\"terms\"]:\n            document_frequencies[term] += 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 3: Parallel IDF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocab_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(vocab_list, chunk_size)\n    idf_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, document_frequencies, len(documents))\n        for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        idf_results = pool.starmap(_compute_idf_chunk, idf_args)\n\n    # Merge IDF results\n    idf = {}\n    for idf_chunk in idf_results:\n        idf.update(idf_chunk)\n\n    # Convert partials to more efficient format for passing to workers\n    doc_tf_dict = {partial[\"doc_id\"]: partial[\"tf\"] for partial in all_partials}\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 4: Parallel Posting List Construction\n    # ========================================================================\n    posting_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        posting_results = pool.map(_build_posting_lists_chunk, posting_args)\n\n    # Merge posting lists\n    inverted_index = {}\n    for posting_chunk in posting_results:\n        inverted_index.update(posting_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 5: Parallel Document Vector and Norm Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Create chunks of doc_tf data\n    doc_tf_list = list(doc_tf_dict.items())\n    doc_tf_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_tf_list, chunk_size)\n    doc_tf_chunk_dicts = [dict(chunk) for chunk in doc_tf_chunks]\n    vec_args = [(chunk_dict, idf) for chunk_dict in doc_tf_chunk_dicts]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, vec_args)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    for vecs, norms in vector_results:\n        doc_vectors.update(vecs)\n        doc_norms.update(norms)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 6: Build Final Index\n    # ========================================================================\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = dict(document_frequencies)\n    index.idf = idf\n    index.inverted_index = inverted_index\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_queries_chunk(\n    queries_chunk: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a chunk of queries sequentially using the shared index.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries_chunk: List of query strings to search\n        index: Shared TF-IDF index (read-only)\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists (one per query)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for query in queries_chunk:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result objects\n        query_results = []\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n\n        results.append(query_results)\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using cosine similarity.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes at the query level:\n    1. Split queries into chunks\n    2. Each worker processes queries independently\n    3. Main process collects and flattens results\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 2x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search (shared read-only)\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results, elapsed_time)\n        results: List of result lists (one per query)\n        elapsed_time: Wall-clock time for batch search\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Create query chunks for parallel processing\n    # Aim for roughly 2-4 chunks per worker for load balancing\n    target_chunks = max(1, num_workers * 2)\n    query_chunk_size = max(1, (len(queries) + target_chunks - 1) // target_chunks)\n    query_chunks = _create_chunks(queries, query_chunk_size)\n\n    # list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass TFIDFIndex:\n    \"\"\"TF-IDF inverted index for similarity search.\"\"\"\n\n    num_documents: int = 0\n    vocabulary: set[str] = field(default_factory=set)\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    idf: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_chunk(documents: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tokenize and compute TF for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects in this chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to:\n        {\n            'doc_id': int,\n            'tf': dict[str, float],\n            'terms': set[str]\n        }\n    \"\"\"\n    partial_results = []\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_terms = set(tf.keys())\n\n        partial_results.append(\n            {\"doc_id\": doc.doc_id, \"tf\": tf, \"terms\": doc_terms}\n        )\n\n    return partial_results\n\n\ndef _compute_idf_chunk(\n    terms_chunk: list[str],\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int],\n    num_documents: int,\n) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute IDF values for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        terms_chunk: List of terms to compute IDF for\n        document_frequencies: Global document frequency counts\n        num_documents: Total number of documents\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to IDF score\n    \"\"\"\n    idf_dict = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = document_frequencies.get(term, 0)\n        if df > 0:\n            idf_dict[term] = math.log(num_documents / df) + 1\n\n    return idf_dict\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build posting lists for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf)\n            - terms_chunk: List of terms to build posting lists for\n            - doc_tf_dict: {doc_id: {term: tf}} lookup\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to posting list [(doc_id, tfidf_score), ...]\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf = args_tuple\n    posting_lists = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        posting_list = []\n\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_dict.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        posting_lists[term] = posting_list\n\n    return posting_lists\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (doc_tf_chunk, idf)\n            - doc_tf_chunk: {doc_id: {term: tf}} for documents in this chunk\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors, doc_norms) dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_chunk, idf = args_tuple\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_chunk.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list[list]:\n    \"\"\"Create chunks of specified size from a list.\"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = None,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes document-independent stages:\n    1. Tokenization and TF computation (parallel)\n    2. Document frequency aggregation (sequential)\n    3. IDF computation (parallel)\n    4. Posting list construction (parallel)\n    5. Document vectors and norms (parallel)\n    6. Merge results (sequential)\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 1.5x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count / 4, min 4)\n        chunk_size: Documents per chunk (default: adaptive based on corpus size)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        cpu_count = os.cpu_count() or 4\n        # Use a reasonable number of workers (not all cores)\n        num_workers = max(4, min(8, cpu_count // 28))\n\n    # Adaptive chunk size: larger documents need larger chunks for efficiency\n    if chunk_size is None:\n        # Aim for ~500-1000 docs per chunk or at least a few chunks per worker\n        avg_word_count = sum(d.word_count for d in documents) / len(documents) if documents else 1\n        # Larger docs → smaller chunks; smaller docs → larger chunks\n        base_chunk = max(50, min(1000, int(100000 / avg_word_count)))\n        chunk_size = base_chunk\n\n    # Validate inputs\n    if not documents:\n        index = TFIDFIndex()\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return ParallelIndexingResult(\n            index=index,\n            elapsed_time=elapsed,\n            num_documents=0,\n            vocabulary_size=0,\n        )\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results_list = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_chunk, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Flatten partial results\n    all_partials = []\n    for partial_chunk in partial_results_list:\n        all_partials.extend(partial_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 2: Sequential Document Frequency Aggregation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocabulary = set()\n    document_frequencies = defaultdict(int)\n\n    for partial in all_partials:\n        vocabulary.update(partial[\"terms\"])\n        for term in partial[\"terms\"]:\n            document_frequencies[term] += 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 3: Parallel IDF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocab_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(vocab_list, chunk_size)\n    idf_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, document_frequencies, len(documents))\n        for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        idf_results = pool.starmap(_compute_idf_chunk, idf_args)\n\n    # Merge IDF results\n    idf = {}\n    for idf_chunk in idf_results:\n        idf.update(idf_chunk)\n\n    # Convert partials to more efficient format for passing to workers\n    doc_tf_dict = {partial[\"doc_id\"]: partial[\"tf\"] for partial in all_partials}\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 4: Parallel Posting List Construction\n    # ========================================================================\n    posting_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        posting_results = pool.map(_build_posting_lists_chunk, posting_args)\n\n    # Merge posting lists\n    inverted_index = {}\n    for posting_chunk in posting_results:\n        inverted_index.update(posting_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 5: Parallel Document Vector and Norm Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Create chunks of doc_tf data\n    doc_tf_list = list(doc_tf_dict.items())\n    doc_tf_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_tf_list, chunk_size)\n    doc_tf_chunk_dicts = [dict(chunk) for chunk in doc_tf_chunks]\n    vec_args = [(chunk_dict, idf) for chunk_dict in doc_tf_chunk_dicts]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, vec_args)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    for vecs, norms in vector_results:\n        doc_vectors.update(vecs)\n        doc_norms.update(norms)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 6: Build Final Index\n    # ========================================================================\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = dict(document_frequencies)\n    index.idf = idf\n    index.inverted_index = inverted_index\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_queries_chunk(\n    queries_chunk: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a chunk of queries sequentially using the shared index.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries_chunk: List of query strings to search\n        index: Shared TF-IDF index (read-only)\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists (one per query)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for query in queries_chunk:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result objects\n        query_results = []\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n\n        results.append(query_results)\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using cosine similarity.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes at the query level:\n    1. Split queries into chunks\n    2. Each worker processes queries independently\n    3. Main process collects and flattens results\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 2x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search (shared read-only)\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results, elapsed_time)\n        results: List of result lists (one per query)\n        elapsed_time: Wall-clock time for batch search\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Create query chunks for parallel processing\n    # Aim for roughly 2-4 chunks per worker for load balancing\n    target_chunks = max(1, num_workers * 2)\n    query_chunk_size = max(1, (len(queries) + target_chunks - 1) // target_chunks)\n    query_chunks = _create_chunks(queries, query_chunk_size)\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers\n    search_args = [(chunk, index, top_k, documents) for chunk in query_chunks]\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        results_list = pool.starmap(_search_queries_chunk, search_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: list of result lists → flat list of result lists\n    all_results = []\n    for chunk_results in results_list:\n        all_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index in parallel\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (parallel)...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform batch search in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    search_results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    query_results = search_results[0] if search_results else []\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(query_results)} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(query_results, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":643,"oldLines":10,"newStart":643,"newLines":10,"lines":["         return [], 0.0"," ","     # Create query chunks for parallel processing","-    # Aim for roughly 2-4 chunks per worker for load balancing","-    target_chunks = max(1, num_workers * 2)","-    query_chunk_size = max(1, (len(queries) + target_chunks - 1) // target_chunks)","-    query_chunks = _create_chunks(queries, query_chunk_size)","+    # Aim for at least 50-100 queries per chunk to amortize overhead","+    # But at least 1-2 chunks per worker","+    queries_per_chunk = max(50, len(queries) // max(1, num_workers * 2))","+    query_chunks = _create_chunks(queries, queries_per_chunk)"," ","     # Prepare arguments for workers","     search_args = [(chunk, index, top_k, documents) for chunk in query_chunks]"]}],"userModified":false,"replaceAll":false}}

inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float] = field(default_factory=dict)\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass SearchResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from similarity search.\"\"\"\n\n    doc_id: int\n    score: float\n    title: str = \"\"\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass IndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n@dataclass\nclass ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"Result from parallel index building.\"\"\"\n\n    index: TFIDFIndex\n    elapsed_time: float\n    num_documents: int\n    vocabulary_size: int\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_chunk(documents: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tokenize and compute TF for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects in this chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to:\n        {\n            'doc_id': int,\n            'tf': dict[str, float],\n            'terms': set[str]\n        }\n    \"\"\"\n    partial_results = []\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_terms = set(tf.keys())\n\n        partial_results.append(\n            {\"doc_id\": doc.doc_id, \"tf\": tf, \"terms\": doc_terms}\n        )\n\n    return partial_results\n\n\ndef _compute_idf_chunk(\n    terms_chunk: list[str],\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int],\n    num_documents: int,\n) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute IDF values for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        terms_chunk: List of terms to compute IDF for\n        document_frequencies: Global document frequency counts\n        num_documents: Total number of documents\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to IDF score\n    \"\"\"\n    idf_dict = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = document_frequencies.get(term, 0)\n        if df > 0:\n            idf_dict[term] = math.log(num_documents / df) + 1\n\n    return idf_dict\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build posting lists for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf)\n            - terms_chunk: List of terms to build posting lists for\n            - doc_tf_dict: {doc_id: {term: tf}} lookup\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to posting list [(doc_id, tfidf_score), ...]\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf = args_tuple\n    posting_lists = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        posting_list = []\n\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_dict.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        posting_lists[term] = posting_list\n\n    return posting_lists\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (doc_tf_chunk, idf)\n            - doc_tf_chunk: {doc_id: {term: tf}} for documents in this chunk\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors, doc_norms) dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_chunk, idf = args_tuple\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_chunk.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list[list]:\n    \"\"\"Create chunks of specified size from a list.\"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = None,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes document-independent stages:\n    1. Tokenization and TF computation (parallel)\n    2. Document frequency aggregation (sequential)\n    3. IDF computation (parallel)\n    4. Posting list construction (parallel)\n    5. Document vectors and norms (parallel)\n    6. Merge results (sequential)\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 1.5x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count / 4, min 4)\n        chunk_size: Documents per chunk (default: adaptive based on corpus size)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        cpu_count = os.cpu_count() or 4\n        # Use a reasonable number of workers (not all cores)\n        num_workers = max(4, min(8, cpu_count // 28))\n\n    # Adaptive chunk size: larger documents need larger chunks for efficiency\n    if chunk_size is None:\n        # Aim for ~500-1000 docs per chunk or at least a few chunks per worker\n        avg_word_count = sum(d.word_count for d in documents) / len(documents) if documents else 1\n        # Larger docs → smaller chunks; smaller docs → larger chunks\n        base_chunk = max(50, min(1000, int(100000 / avg_word_count)))\n        chunk_size = base_chunk\n\n    # Validate inputs\n    if not documents:\n        index = TFIDFIndex()\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return ParallelIndexingResult(\n            index=index,\n            elapsed_time=elapsed,\n            num_documents=0,\n            vocabulary_size=0,\n        )\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results_list = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_chunk, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Flatten partial results\n    all_partials = []\n    for partial_chunk in partial_results_list:\n        all_partials.extend(partial_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 2: Sequential Document Frequency Aggregation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocabulary = set()\n    document_frequencies = defaultdict(int)\n\n    for partial in all_partials:\n        vocabulary.update(partial[\"terms\"])\n        for term in partial[\"terms\"]:\n            document_frequencies[term] += 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 3: Parallel IDF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocab_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(vocab_list, chunk_size)\n    idf_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, document_frequencies, len(documents))\n        for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        idf_results = pool.starmap(_compute_idf_chunk, idf_args)\n\n    # Merge IDF results\n    idf = {}\n    for idf_chunk in idf_results:\n        idf.update(idf_chunk)\n\n    # Convert partials to more efficient format for passing to workers\n    doc_tf_dict = {partial[\"doc_id\"]: partial[\"tf\"] for partial in all_partials}\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 4: Parallel Posting List Construction\n    # ========================================================================\n    posting_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        posting_results = pool.map(_build_posting_lists_chunk, posting_args)\n\n    # Merge posting lists\n    inverted_index = {}\n    for posting_chunk in posting_results:\n        inverted_index.update(posting_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 5: Parallel Document Vector and Norm Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Create chunks of doc_tf data\n    doc_tf_list = list(doc_tf_dict.items())\n    doc_tf_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_tf_list, chunk_size)\n    doc_tf_chunk_dicts = [dict(chunk) for chunk in doc_tf_chunks]\n    vec_args = [(chunk_dict, idf) for chunk_dict in doc_tf_chunk_dicts]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, vec_args)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    for vecs, norms in vector_results:\n        doc_vectors.update(vecs)\n        doc_norms.update(norms)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result objects\n        query_results = []\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n\n        results.append(query_results)\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # Split queries into chunks\n    2. Each worker processes queries independently\n    3. Main process collects and flattens results\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 2x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search (shared read-only)\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results, elapsed_time)\n        results: List of result lists (one per query)\n        elapsed_time: Wall-clock time for batch search\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Create query chunks for parallel processing\n    # Aim for at least 50-100 queries per chunk to amortize overhead\n    # But at least 1-2 chunks per worker\n    queries_per_chunk = max(50, len(queries) // max(1, num_workers * 2))\n    query_chunks = _create_chunks(queries, queries_per_chunk)\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers\n    search_args = [(chunk, index, top_k, documents) for chunk in query_chunks]\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        results_list = pool.starmap(_search_queries_chunk, search_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: list of result lists → flat list of result lists\n    all_results = []\n    for chunk_results in results_list:\n        all_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Perform batch search in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    search_results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    query_results = search_results[0] if search_results else []\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(query_results)} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(query_results, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# inverted_index: dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_vectors: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = field(default_factory=dict)\n    doc_norms: dict[int, float] = ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_chunk(documents: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tokenize and compute TF for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects in this chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to:\n        {\n            'doc_id': int,\n            'tf': dict[str, float],\n            'terms': set[str]\n        }\n    \"\"\"\n    partial_results = []\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_terms = set(tf.keys())\n\n        partial_results.append(\n            {\"doc_id\": doc.doc_id, \"tf\": tf, \"terms\": doc_terms}\n        )\n\n    return partial_results\n\n\ndef _compute_idf_chunk(\n    terms_chunk: list[str],\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int],\n    num_documents: int,\n) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute IDF values for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        terms_chunk: List of terms to compute IDF for\n        document_frequencies: Global document frequency counts\n        num_documents: Total number of documents\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to IDF score\n    \"\"\"\n    idf_dict = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = document_frequencies.get(term, 0)\n        if df > 0:\n            idf_dict[term] = math.log(num_documents / df) + 1\n\n    return idf_dict\n\n\ndef _build_posting_lists_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> dict[str, list[tuple[int, float]]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build posting lists for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf)\n            - terms_chunk: List of terms to build posting lists for\n            - doc_tf_dict: {doc_id: {term: tf}} lookup\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to posting list [(doc_id, tfidf_score), ...]\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf = args_tuple\n    posting_lists = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        posting_list = []\n\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_dict.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        posting_lists[term] = posting_list\n\n    return posting_lists\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (doc_tf_chunk, idf)\n            - doc_tf_chunk: {doc_id: {term: tf}} for documents in this chunk\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors, doc_norms) dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_chunk, idf = args_tuple\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_chunk.items():\n        doc_vector = {}\n        norm_squared = 0.0\n\n        for term, tf in tf_dict.items():\n            tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n            doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors, doc_norms\n\n\ndef _create_chunks(items: list, chunk_size: int) -> list[list]:\n    \"\"\"Create chunks of specified size from a list.\"\"\"\n    chunks = []\n    for i in range(0, len(items), chunk_size):\n        chunks.append(items[i : i + chunk_size])\n    return chunks\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n    documents: list[Document],\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    chunk_size: int = None,\n) -> ParallelIndexingResult:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build TF-IDF inverted index in parallel.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes document-independent stages:\n    1. Tokenization and TF computation (parallel)\n    2. Document frequency aggregation (sequential)\n    3. IDF computation (parallel)\n    4. Posting list construction (parallel)\n    5. Document vectors and norms (parallel)\n    6. Merge results (sequential)\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 1.5x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects to index\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count / 4, min 4)\n        chunk_size: Documents per chunk (default: adaptive based on corpus size)\n\n    Returns:\n        ParallelIndexingResult with the built index and timing info\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        cpu_count = os.cpu_count() or 4\n        # Use a reasonable number of workers (not all cores)\n        num_workers = max(4, min(8, cpu_count // 28))\n\n    # Adaptive chunk size: larger documents need larger chunks for efficiency\n    if chunk_size is None:\n        # Aim for ~500-1000 docs per chunk or at least a few chunks per worker\n        avg_word_count = sum(d.word_count for d in documents) / len(documents) if documents else 1\n        # Larger docs → smaller chunks; smaller docs → larger chunks\n        base_chunk = max(50, min(1000, int(100000 / avg_word_count)))\n        chunk_size = base_chunk\n\n    # Validate inputs\n    if not documents:\n        index = TFIDFIndex()\n        elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n        return ParallelIndexingResult(\n            index=index,\n            elapsed_time=elapsed,\n            num_documents=0,\n            vocabulary_size=0,\n        )\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 1: Parallel Tokenization and TF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    doc_chunks = _create_chunks(documents, chunk_size)\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        partial_results_list = pool.map(_tokenize_documents_chunk, doc_chunks)\n\n    # Flatten partial results\n    all_partials = []\n    for partial_chunk in partial_results_list:\n        all_partials.extend(partial_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 2: Sequential Document Frequency Aggregation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocabulary = set()\n    document_frequencies = defaultdict(int)\n\n    for partial in all_partials:\n        vocabulary.update(partial[\"terms\"])\n        for term in partial[\"terms\"]:\n            document_frequencies[term] += 1\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 3: Parallel IDF Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    vocab_list = list(vocabulary)\n    vocab_chunks = _create_chunks(vocab_list, chunk_size)\n    idf_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, document_frequencies, len(documents))\n        for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        idf_results = pool.starmap(_compute_idf_chunk, idf_args)\n\n    # Merge IDF results\n    idf = {}\n    for idf_chunk in idf_results:\n        idf.update(idf_chunk)\n\n    # Convert partials to more efficient format for passing to workers\n    doc_tf_dict = {partial[\"doc_id\"]: partial[\"tf\"] for partial in all_partials}\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 4: Parallel Posting List Construction\n    # ========================================================================\n    posting_args = [\n        (vocab_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf) for vocab_chunk in vocab_chunks\n    ]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        posting_results = pool.map(_build_posting_lists_chunk, posting_args)\n\n    # Merge posting lists\n    inverted_index = {}\n    for posting_chunk in posting_results:\n        inverted_index.update(posting_chunk)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 5: Parallel Document Vector and Norm Computation\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Create chunks of doc_tf data\n    doc_tf_list = list(doc_tf_dict.items())\n    doc_tf_chunks = _create_chunks(doc_tf_list, chunk_size)\n    doc_tf_chunk_dicts = [dict(chunk) for chunk in doc_tf_chunks]\n    vec_args = [(chunk_dict, idf) for chunk_dict in doc_tf_chunk_dicts]\n\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        vector_results = pool.map(_build_doc_vectors_chunk, vec_args)\n\n    # Merge document vectors and norms\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n    for vecs, norms in vector_results:\n        doc_vectors.update(vecs)\n        doc_norms.update(norms)\n\n    # ========================================================================\n    # Stage 6: Build Final Index\n    # ========================================================================\n    index = TFIDFIndex()\n    index.num_documents = len(documents)\n    index.vocabulary = vocabulary\n    index.document_frequencies = dict(document_frequencies)\n    index.idf = idf\n    index.inverted_index = inverted_index\n    index.doc_vectors = doc_vectors\n    index.doc_norms = doc_norms\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return ParallelIndexingResult(\n        index=index,\n        elapsed_time=elapsed,\n        num_documents=len(documents),\n        vocabulary_size=len(vocabulary),\n    )\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _search_queries_chunk(\n    queries_chunk: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n) -> list[list[SearchResult]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search a chunk of queries sequentially using the shared index.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        queries_chunk: List of query strings to search\n        index: Shared TF-IDF index (read-only)\n        top_k: Number of top results per query\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        List of result lists (one per query)\n    \"\"\"\n    results = []\n\n    for query in queries_chunk:\n        # Tokenize query\n        query_tokens = tokenize(query)\n        if not query_tokens:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        # Compute query TF\n        query_tf = compute_term_frequencies(query_tokens)\n\n        # Compute query TF-IDF vector\n        query_vector = {}\n        query_norm_squared = 0.0\n        for term, tf in query_tf.items():\n            if term in index.idf:\n                tfidf = tf * index.idf[term]\n                query_vector[term] = tfidf\n                query_norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        if not query_vector:\n            results.append([])\n            continue\n\n        query_norm = math.sqrt(query_norm_squared)\n\n        # Find candidate documents\n        candidate_docs = set()\n        for term in query_vector:\n            if term in index.inverted_index:\n                for doc_id, _ in index.inverted_index[term]:\n                    candidate_docs.add(doc_id)\n\n        # Compute cosine similarity\n        scores = []\n        for doc_id in candidate_docs:\n            doc_vector = index.doc_vectors.get(doc_id, {})\n            doc_norm = index.doc_norms.get(doc_id, 0)\n\n            if doc_norm == 0:\n                continue\n\n            # Dot product\n            dot_product = sum(\n                query_vector.get(term, 0) * doc_vector.get(term, 0)\n                for term in query_vector\n            )\n\n            # Cosine similarity\n            similarity = dot_product / (query_norm * doc_norm)\n            scores.append((doc_id, similarity))\n\n        # Get top-k results\n        top_results = nlargest(top_k, scores, key=lambda x: x[1])\n\n        # Build result objects\n        query_results = []\n        doc_titles = {d.doc_id: d.title for d in documents} if documents else {}\n        for doc_id, score in top_results:\n            query_results.append(\n                SearchResult(\n                    doc_id=doc_id,\n                    score=score,\n                    title=doc_titles.get(doc_id, f\"Document {doc_id}\"),\n                )\n            )\n\n        results.append(query_results)\n\n    return results\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Search\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef batch_search_parallel(\n    queries: list[str],\n    index: TFIDFIndex,\n    top_k: int = 10,\n    num_workers: int = None,\n    documents: list[Document] = None,  # noqa: RUF013\n) -> tuple[list[list[SearchResult]], float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Search for multiple queries in parallel using cosine similarity.\n\n    This implementation parallelizes at the query level:\n    1. Split queries into chunks\n    2. Each worker processes queries independently\n    3. Main process collects and flattens results\n\n    Performance targets:\n    - 2x speedup over sequential with 4 workers\n\n    Args:\n        queries: List of search query strings\n        index: TF-IDF index to search (shared read-only)\n        top_k: Number of top results per query (default: 10)\n        num_workers: Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\n        documents: Optional list of documents for title lookup\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (results, elapsed_time)\n        results: List of result lists (one per query)\n        elapsed_time: Wall-clock time for batch search\n    \"\"\"\n    start_time = time.perf_counter()\n\n    if num_workers is None:\n        num_workers = os.cpu_count() or 4\n\n    if not queries:\n        return [], 0.0\n\n    # Create query chunks for parallel processing\n    # Aim for at least 50-100 queries per chunk to amortize overhead\n    # But at least 1-2 chunks per worker\n    queries_per_chunk = max(50, len(queries) // max(1, num_workers * 2))\n    query_chunks = _create_chunks(queries, queries_per_chunk)\n\n    # Prepare arguments for workers\n    search_args = [(chunk, index, top_k, documents) for chunk in query_chunks]\n\n    # Execute searches in parallel\n    with Pool(num_workers) as pool:\n        results_list = pool.starmap(_search_queries_chunk, search_args)\n\n    # Flatten results: list of result lists → flat list of result lists\n    all_results = []\n    for chunk_results in results_list:\n        all_results.extend(chunk_results)\n\n    elapsed = time.perf_counter() - start_time\n\n    return all_results, elapsed\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Main Entry Point\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef main():\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(\n        description=\"Parallel TF-IDF indexing and search\"\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--corpus\", type=str, default=None, help=\"Path to corpus\")\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-docs\",\n        type=int,\n        default=5000,\n        help=\"Number of documents to generate if no corpus\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--num-workers\",\n        type=int,\n        default=None,\n        help=\"Number of parallel workers (default: CPU count)\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\n        \"--query\",\n        type=str,\n        default=\"machine learning algorithm\",\n        help=\"Search query\",\n    )\n    parser.add_argument(\"--top-k\", type=int, default=10, help=\"Number of top results\")\n\n    args = parser.parse_args()\n\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"Parallel TF-IDF Search Engine\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n\n    # Load or generate corpus\n    if args.corpus:\n        print(f\"\\nLoading corpus from {args.corpus}...\")\n        documents = load_corpus(args.corpus)\n    else:\n        print(f\"\\nGenerating {args.num_docs} documents...\")\n        documents = generate_corpus(args.num_docs, seed=42)\n\n    print(f\"Corpus size: {len(documents)} documents\")\n    total_words = sum(d.word_count for d in documents)\n    print(f\"Total words: {total_words:,}\")\n\n    # Build index in parallel\n    print(\"\\nBuilding TF-IDF index (parallel)...\")\n    result = build_tfidf_index_parallel(\n        documents, num_workers=args.num_workers\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\nIndex built in {result.elapsed_time:.3f} seconds\")\n    print(f\"Vocabulary size: {result.vocabulary_size:,} terms\")\n    print(f\"Documents indexed: {result.num_documents}\")\n\n    # Perform batch search in parallel\n    print(f\"\\nSearching for: '{args.query}'\")\n    print(\"-\" * 60)\n\n    search_results, search_time = batch_search_parallel(\n        [args.query],\n        result.index,\n        args.top_k,\n        num_workers=args.num_workers,\n        documents=documents,\n    )\n\n    query_results = search_results[0] if search_results else []\n\n    print(f\"Search completed in {search_time*1000:.2f} ms\")\n    print(f\"\\nTop {len(query_results)} results:\")\n    for i, res in enumerate(query_results, 1):\n        print(f\"  {i}. [{res.score:.4f}] {res.title} (doc_id: {res.doc_id})\")\n\n    return result\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    main()\n","structuredPatch":[{"oldStart":400,"oldLines":76,"newStart":400,"newLines":73,"lines":["             vocabulary_size=0,","         )"," ","-    # list[str]) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"Compute normalized term frequencies for a list of tokens.\"\"\"\n    if not tokens:\n        return {}\n\n    counts = defaultdict(int)\n    for token in tokens:\n        counts[token] += 1\n\n    total = len(tokens)\n    return {term: count / total for term, count in counts.items()}\n\n\n# ============================================================================\n# Parallel Index Building Helper Functions\n# ============================================================================\n\n\ndef _tokenize_documents_chunk(documents: list[Document]) -> dict:\n    \"\"\"\n    Tokenize and compute TF for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        documents: List of Document objects in this chunk\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping doc_id to:\n        {\n            'doc_id': int,\n            'tf': dict[str, float],\n            'terms': set[str]\n        }\n    \"\"\"\n    partial_results = []\n\n    for doc in documents:\n        text = doc.title + \" \" + doc.content\n        tokens = tokenize(text)\n        tf = compute_term_frequencies(tokens)\n        doc_terms = set(tf.keys())\n\n        partial_results.append(\n            {\"doc_id\": doc.doc_id, \"tf\": tf, \"terms\": doc_terms}\n        )\n\n    return partial_results\n\n\ndef _compute_idf_chunk(\n    terms_chunk: list[str],\n    document_frequencies: dict[str, int],\n    num_documents: int,\n) -> dict[str, float]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Compute IDF values for a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        terms_chunk: List of terms to compute IDF for\n        document_frequencies: Global document frequency counts\n        num_documents: Total number of documents\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to IDF score\n    \"\"\"\n    idf_dict = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        df = document_frequencies.get(term, 0)\n        if df > 0:\n            idf_dict[term] = a chunk of terms.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf)\n            - terms_chunk: List of terms to build posting lists for\n            - doc_tf_dict: {doc_id: {term: tf}} lookup\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Dictionary mapping term to posting list [(doc_id, tfidf_score), ...]\n    \"\"\"\n    terms_chunk, doc_tf_dict, idf = args_tuple\n    posting_lists = {}\n\n    for term in terms_chunk:\n        posting_list = []\n\n        for doc_id, tf_dict in doc_tf_dict.items():\n            if term in tf_dict:\n                tf = tf_dict[term]\n                tfidf = tf * idf.get(term, 0)\n                posting_list.append((doc_id, tfidf))\n\n        # Sort by score descending for efficient top-k retrieval\n        posting_list.sort(key=lambda x: x[1], reverse=True)\n        posting_lists[term] = posting_list\n\n    return posting_lists\n\n\ndef _build_doc_vectors_chunk(\n    args_tuple: tuple,\n) -> tuple[dict[int, dict[str, float]], dict[int, float]]:\n    \"\"\"\n    Build document vectors and compute norms for a chunk of documents.\n\n    This is a worker function executed in parallel.\n\n    Args:\n        args_tuple: (doc_tf_chunk, idf)\n            - doc_tf_chunk: {doc_id: {term: tf}} for documents in this chunk\n            - idf: Global IDF dictionary\n\n    Returns:\n        Tuple of (doc_vectors, doc_norms) dictionaries\n    \"\"\"\n    doc_tf_chunk, idf = args_tuple\n    doc_vectors = {}\n    doc_norms = {}\n\n    for doc_vector[term] = tfidf\n            norm_squared += tfidf * tfidf\n\n        doc_vectors[doc_id] = doc_vector\n        doc_norms[doc_id] = math.sqrt(norm_squared)\n\n    return doc_vectors,
